## Document 058: 261005_daiwa_TW-CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/261005_daiwa_TW-CCL.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: After market hours on 5 October 2026, EMC, TUC, and ITEQ released their 
monthly sales results. Our read-through is positive as the three key CCL vendors beat 
sales expectations by 5-15% on better pricing and on-going spec upgrades. Our thesis is 
that the strong AI demand is causing a spill-over impact on the whole CCL industry with 
low-end CCL having c.100% price hike in 2026E and high-end CCL seeing a 40-50% price 
hike due to cost inflation in 2026E. Recent data points showed this thesis remains valid. 
Looking ahead, we expect high-end/low-end CCL to see additional c.10%/20% ASP 
increases in 4Q26E, which could be the key share price catalyst. We believe that recent 
negatives news (including the impact of PTFE and CPO/NPO on the industry) are 
overdone. The impact on high-end CCL from these new technologies is likely to be limited 
from the point of view of Taiwan CCL companies. As such, we remain positive that the 
three names in our CCL coverage will ride on the decent price hike trend given favourable 
supply-demand dynamics.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts. Note: Closing prices as of 5 October 2026.

• EMC (2383 TT, TWD5,700, Buy [1]) 
EMC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD21.5bn (+7% MoM, +169% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD60.9bn; +29% QoQ, +142% YoY) reaching 112% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD54.4bn) and 110% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD55.5bn). We attribute 
the revenue strength to the higher-than-expected price hike and on-going spec upgrades 
(leading to higher ASPs). Management attributes c.50% of the MoM increase in revenue in 
September to the price hike and the other 50% came to product mix optimisation. Looking 
ahead, the company doesn’t rule out the potential to increase its ASP again in 4Q26 (after 
the first time in 2Q26) in order to reflect rising raw material prices since 2Q26. As such, we 
expect revenue to grow at least high teens again on the potential price head as well as 
ongoing spec upgrades.

EMC sees limited impact from PTFE and HC glass-fibre-free CCL. Based on our 
understanding, PTFE is still under certification accounting for only limited layers inside 
specified high-end PCB as of now. As such, its impact on the high-end CCL TAM should be 
limited. As for HC glass-fibre-free CCL, the rigidity of PCB using this type of CCL could be 
a key concern to us.

Lastly, regarding the recent Co-Packaged Optics/Near-Packaged Optics (CPO/NPO) 
news’s negative impact on the CCL TAM, we also believe it to be limited given the likely 
adoption date for CPO of 2030, based on EMC’s forecast. Besides, we believe the PCB 
board inside CPO scale-up switches at that time would still need to support an 800G/1.6T 
transmission speed (or even lower) which would still require high-end PCB transmission 
although the highest transmission bandwidth can increase to 3.2T via optics. Lastly, EMC 
believes the spec of CCL won’t downgrade to M4 in the worst-case scenario but would be 
at least M6. Last but not least, we believe this kind of CPO-connected AI server might only

shouldn’t be large.

Given its leading position in the CCL industry and the likely imbalance between supply and 
demand in 2027, we remain positive view on EMC. We have a Buy (1) rating on EMC, with 
a 12-month TP of TWD7,200, based on a target PER of 38x applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected AI server demand.

• TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,780, Buy [1]) 
TUC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD6.1bn (+4% MoM, +115% YoY), which led to its 
3Q26 sales (TWD17.9bn; +26% QoQ, +123% YoY) reaching 103% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD17.4bn) and 105% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD17.1bn). Looking 
ahead, we expect TUC to deliver at least QoQ growth in 4Q26 given 1) a price hike along 
with its peers for high-end CCL (eg. M8) and 2) some shipment delay from 3Q26 given 
flooding in Southeast Asia. For the longer term, the company is promising a continual spec 
upgrade trend in 2027 with new projects using M9-grade CCL (including low dk1, low dk2, 
and Q-glass version) in 2027 for high-end switches, AI servers, and optical transceivers. 
Considering the insufficient capacity in the CCL industry, we expect TUC to further optimise 
its product mix after acquiring more high-end CCL orders in future quarters (vs. a single 
major AI server client in 2026). We have a Buy (1) rating on TUC, with a 12-month TP of 
TWD2,320, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Key 
downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for switches/AI servers, and 2) market 
share loss to CCL peers.

• ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD751, Buy [1]) 
ITEQ’s September 2026 revenue was TWD5.8bn (+15% MoM, +111% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD15.6bn; +35% QoQ, +102% YoY) reaching 114% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD13.7bn) and 108% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD14.5bn). The number 
for 3Q26 was higher than our estimate due likely to a stronger-than-expected price hike 
(c.30% in 3Q26 vs. expectation of 20%). Looking ahead to 4Q26, we expect the company 
to further raise its pricing by 20-30% to factor in cost inflation in raw materials (E-glass) 
and the supply-demand gap. The company also expects the rising supply of e-glass (which 
could lift its utilisation rate from the current level of 75%) to lift its gross margin more 
significantly. For 2027, the company is excited about the new CPU adoption driving rising 
M7 CCL usage and is working on M9 certification with new end customers, which could 
become a key growth driver in 2028. We have a Buy (1) rating on ITEQ. Key downside 
risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

## Document 070: 261006_daiwa_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/261006_daiwa_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We believe an improving product mix, driven by rising 
Nvidia’s CPU contribution, to be a key near-term share price catalyst.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. After Kinsus released its August 
preliminary results on 23 September, we note that we had underestimated 
the 3Q26E gross margin expansion on product optimisation and price hike 
scale. The results imply a GM of 33% in August (vs. 26% in 2Q26). We 
therefore lift our 3Q-4Q26E gross margin to 33.2-35.1% (from 28.9-30.3%) 
to reflect an improving product mix, including greater CPU substrate 
contribution. We believe the gross margin of high-end chipset substrate 
may be c.50%, supporting margin expansion. We also expect a c.10% QoQ 
price hike for both BT and ABF substrate in 3Q26, above Kinsus’ guidance 
of a high-single-digit% QoQ, making these substrates the other growth 
driver. We see further gross margin expansion in 2027-28 on the ongoing 
price hike trend and increasing high-end ABF substrate product mix.

Business outlook. We believe several favourable trends lie ahead. First, 
we see a rising contribution from high-end GPU and CPU ABF substrates. 
We believe Nvidia’s CPU currently accounts for 5-10% of total revenue, 
with Kinsus holding a 70-80% market share. We also expect Kinsus to 
acquire a teens% market share in AMD’s Venice, which should gradually 
ramp up from 4Q26. These will likely become key growth drivers of the ABF 
substrate segment in 2027, in our view. We believe the ramp-up progress 
for GPU substrate for these two clients is slightly slower than that for CPU. 
Second, we believe Kinsus will benefit from the rising BT substrate demand 
for optical transceivers. In our view, migration to on-board optics (OBO) and 
NPO/CPO will require ABF substrate beneath the optics engine (OE) in 
OBO; the OE and IC will need a bigger ABF substrate as we move to the 
NPO/CPO generation. As such, we expect the total ABF substrate demand 
to increase with rising adoption of optics in AI servers. Lastly, we expect the 
adoption of multiple-core ABF from 2028 to improve the rigidity and 
transmission performance before the official launch of ceramic and glass-
core substrate, which we expect to increase the ABF TAM as well.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 19-29% to 
reflect the better-than-expected gross margin in August. We reaffirm our 
Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP to TWD1,580, based on an unchanged 
target PER of 36x (high end of 3-year-trading range of 10-36x), applied to 
the 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 27-57% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more bullish view on its gross margin expansion.

6 October 2026

3Q26 results preview: several favourable trends ahead

➢ Rising high-end ABF substrate likely to drive blended ASP increase   
➢ Multicore and optics likely to be the next growth drivers 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD1,580

Company profile  
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and mainland China.

## Document 071: 261006_daiwa_winway

Source PDF: all report pdf/261006_daiwa_winway.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Winway reported September revenue of TWD1.25bn (-26.7% MoM; +87.7% 
YoY), brining 3Q26 revenue to a record high of TWD4.56bn (+29.3% QoQ; +152.6% YoY). 
The 3Q26 top-line result beat market consensus by 6.7% but missed Daiwa’s estimate by 
9.1%. Following August’s exceptionally strong top line (TWD1.71bn), we had factored in a 
higher shipment run-rate for September. However, the miss stems primarily from the 
lumpiness of probe-card deliveries (high concentration to a single customer) and customer 
qualification timelines, rather than order cancellations or share loss.

We forecast 4Q26 revenue to track flattish to slightly down QoQ, reflecting lower probe-
card shipment volumes compared to 3Q26, alongside typical year-end pull-in deceleration 
from customers. On profitability, we maintain our view that 2Q26 marked the trough for 
gross margin this year. As the revenue contribution from lower-margin MEMS probe cards 
declines, a more favourable product mix should drive a steady QoQ gross margin recovery, 
which may drive gross margin to reach 40% earlier than market expectations.

Although we remain positive on Winway’s structural growth profile, near-term catalysts 
appear muted. This reflects the revenue lumpiness in probe cards, while additional socket 
capacity will not come online until March 2027—pushing the corresponding revenue 
recognition into 2Q27. We will revisit our financial model upon the company’s 3Q26 results 
release. We currently have a Buy (1) rating on Winway with a 12-month TP of TWD10,000.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
September  Sales  +22% MoM/
+206% YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• September revenue was NT$6,860mn (22% MoM/+206% YoY), a record
high.
• 3Q26 revenue came in at  NT$17,926mn (+32% QoQ/+180% YoY),
significantly higher than  our estimate at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130%
YoY) and consensus at NT$14,555mn (+8% QoQ/+127% YoY).

Our view:
• We believe that the strength in 3Q26 was mainly driven by the
semiconductor and photonics business, especially SL T, metrology,
transceiver/CPO testing, etc.
• We think that Chroma might start to ship FT handlers and ovens in small
volume from late 2026/early 2027, which represent new business
opportunities for the coming years.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 133: AI算力需求爆发，联讯仪器前三季度净利润同比预增1866%至2073% ｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI算力需求爆发，联讯仪器前三季度净利润同比预增1866%至2073% ｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

联讯仪器预计 2026 年前三季度净利润达 19 亿⾄ 21 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 1866% ⾄ 2073% 。业绩
⼤幅增⻓主要受业务规模扩⼤、营收显著提升推动。随着 AI 算⼒建设带动 800G 、 1.6T ⾼速
光模块加速发展，光通信产业链测试设备需求有望持续增⻓。
摘要：
AI 算⼒基础设施建设持续升温，带动⾼速光通信产业链需求增⻓，联讯仪器前三季度业绩迎来爆
发式增⻓。
10 ⽉ 12 ⽇，联讯仪器发布 2026 年前三季度业绩预告，预计实现归属于上市公司股东的净利润 19
亿元⾄ 21 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 1866% ⾄ 2073% ；预计扣除⾮经常性损益后的净利润为 18 亿元⾄ 20 亿
元，同⽐增⻓ 1837% ⾄ 2052% 。
从利润构成看，扣⾮净利润与归⺟净利润的增幅基本⼀致，表明业绩增⻓主要体现在扣除⾮经
常性损益后的利润中。具体增⻓动因仍需结合公司后续公告中的业务情况进⼀步分析。
不过，业绩变化的对⽐基数较低。 2025 年前三季度，公司归⺟净利润约 9664 万元，扣⾮净利润

约 9293 万元。

写评论
AI 算⼒建设带动⾼速光通信测试需求
随着⼤模型训练与推理需求增⻓，数据中⼼对⾼速互联的需求持续上升， 800G 、 1.6T 等⾼速光
模块加速研发和量产，带动测试设备需求增⻓。光模块和光芯⽚⼚商扩产，也进⼀步增加了研
发验证、性能检测和质量控制等环节的测试需求。
联讯仪器的通信测试仪器及光电⼦器件测试设备，正处于⾼速光通信产业链的关键检测环节。
随着产品迭代和产能扩张，相关测试需求有望进⼀步增⻓，但 AI 基础设施建设对公司业绩的具体
贡献仍需结合产品销售及下游需求判断。
公司公告指出，此次业绩增⻓主要得益于业务规模扩⼤和营业收⼊同⽐显著提升。
仪器设备企业通常需要承担较⾼的前期研发和⽣产投⼊。当收⼊增⻓快于固定成本增⻓时，新
增收⼊便可能带来更⼤的利润增量，形成经营杠杆效应。

## Document 157: Capital Market Outlook (2026-09-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-09-28).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The Five Shocks of 2026—And the Extraordinary Resilience of the U.S. Economy
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
This is the year the consensus got mugged.
Rewind to the start of the year. The market consensus was that the U.S. economy was
set to slow. The Fed was set to cut interest rates. Corporate earnings were set to grow—

but not explode. Energy prices would remain tame. And the odds looked good that the
Republicans would control the House, the Senate, or both, following the midterm election.
Nine months on, the landscape looks starkly different. Defined by five shocks—energy,
interest rates, earnings, growth, and politics—2026 looks nothing like the year investors
thought they were buying on January 1.
Shock 1: Diesel Matters. While global oil prices have moved higher since the start of

the U.S./Iran war on February 28, oil prices have yet to surpass their previous record high
because the world has released some 400 million barrels from strategic reserves this year,
China has curbed its demand for oil imports, and the U.S. has goosed its energy exports to
record highs.
But here is the problem: While gasoline powers the American consumer, diesel powers the
American economy—and diesel prices are now sitting at record highs. Diesel supplies have

been hammered by wars in the Middle East and Ukraine that have sharply reduced refining
capacity. According to the International Energy Agency, by August, exports of diesel from
refiners in the Middle East had fallen by a quarter from prewar levels. Meanwhile, Ukraine
has hit 24 of Russia’s 34 largest refineries, reducing supplies from one of the world’s
largest diesel exporters. The upshot: U.S. diesel prices have soared from $3.55 a gallon
at the start of the year to roughly $6.52 a gallon. While crude oil trades for around $100

a barrel, the retail equivalent of a barrel of diesel now costs more than $260. The diesel
shock of 2026 was the last thing investors saw coming—and remains a key impediment to
structurally lower inflation.
Shock 2: From Interest Rate Cuts to Rate Hikes. Perhaps no other consensus view
entering 2026 has been turned more dramatically on its head than expectations for the
Fed. Heading into 2026, markets were pricing in rate cuts on expectations of a cooling

labor market, moderating inflation, and slower economic growth. The Fed easing cycle was
expected to continue in 2026. In January 2026, futures showed a roughly 50% chance of a
cut by March.
Expectations, however, flipped in late February with the start of the U.S./Iran war. The
Middle East energy shock reignited inflationary pressures, which, combined with stronger-
than-expected U.S. economic growth and a tightening labor market, have demolished the

odds of easier money in 2026. Short- and long-term Treasury yields rose sharply last week
in the wake of the S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI)
which pointed to the strongest business expansion in more than five years and the fastest
employment growth in over four years, while also reinforcing expectations that the Fed
will maintain a restrictive policy stance for longer. Markets were expecting two to three
quarter-point rate cuts in 2026; instead, the Fed has already raised rates once this year and

is expected to keep raising rates over the near term.
Shock 3: The Earnings Boom No One Saw Coming. Not all the shocks of 2026 are
negative. Indeed, stronger-than-expected corporate earnings have been among the most
positive factors of this year. Corporate America entered the year facing questions about
tariffs, AI-related capital expenditure spending, the health of the U.S. consumer, operating
leverage and the U.S. dollar outlook. At the start of the year, Wall Street was expecting

roughly $290 of S&P 500 earnings per share for 2026, per Bloomberg estimates. But by
September 21, the estimate had risen by $50, to $340 per share.
Investment Implications
While some of these shocks may
contribute to periods of market
volatility, the combination of strong
earnings growth, robust domestic
demand, and ongoing capital
investment suggests that the
fundamental backdrop for Equities
remains constructive.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Earnings have been upgraded and underpinned by several factors, including robust final 
domestic demand in the U.S., a pickup in global growth, AI-related capital spending, 
productivity gains, and enhanced operating leverage. All these variables have helped deliver 
an earnings “shock” to Wall Street. And as an aside, yes, headline profit growth has been 
inflated by strong technology earnings and one-off investment gains from Amazon and 
Alphabet. That said, the S&P 500’s earnings story remains extraordinary. According to 
FactSet, the 493 companies excluding the Magnificent 7 1 
posted 31.8% earnings growth in 
Q2, the strongest level since late 2021.

Shock 4: An Economy with more Horsepower than the Headlines Suggest. That 
corporate profits have exceeded expectations this ye
ar is due in no small part to an economy 
that continues to surprise to the upside. The Fed was supposed to cut rates this year to 
keep the economy humming and prevent it from losing steam. But rather than limp through 
2026, the economy has leaped ahead. Yes, real gross domestic product (GDP) growth of 
2.1% in Q1 and 1.5% in Q2 are hardly blockbuster annualized rates of growth. However, the 
figures belie the underlying strength of the U.S. economy, with real final domestic demand

(which strips out trade) running at a 4.2% annual rate in Q2. According to the latest reading 
of the Atlanta Fed GDP tracker, Q3 real GDP grow
th is running at a red-hot rate of 5.1%. 
Slowdown? What slowdown?
Resilient consumer spending, robust capital investment, record leve
ls of new business 
formation, and strong foreign direct investment inflows have combined to stoke growth 
in the $32 trillion behemoth called the U.S. economy—the most resilient and dynamic 
economy in the world.
Shock 5: The Unexpected Washington Reset. The last shock is still in the making, but 
given current trends, the odds are rising that the Democrats could control both the House 
and Senate follow
ing the midterm elections. To this point, in January, and per Kalshi, the 
Democrats had a 71% shot at the House and an 18% chance of taking the Senate. Fast-
forward to today, and the odds are a 90% chance that the Democrats take the House and a 
60% chance that they take the Senate.
How all of this plays out remains to be seen—there are some 36 days before the November 
vote, and anything can happen. The market base case remains that the Democrats take the 
House, and the Senate is a toss-up—or too close to call. But with the Democrats expected to 
control at least one chamber of Congress, inv
estors can expect more volatility and uncertainty 
about negotiations ov
er the budget, debt ceiling, defense spending and AI regulation. Another 
political wildcard to watch: the changing dynamics within the Democratic Party.
The Biggest Shock of All for 2026? Pe
rhaps it’s not $6 diesel, rising interest rates, 
solid growth, booming profits or shifting politics. Rather, it’s the ability of the U.S. 
economy to absorb the unexpected, not buckle under stress, and continue generating  wealth 
for investors. To wit, the S&P 500 is on pace for its fourth
 consecutive year of double-digit 
returns.

### Page 4

4 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
2026 Has, So Far, Defied the Traditional Midterm Year Playbook
Jordy Fuentes, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Current Landscape. To no surprise, market volatility persisted in 2026. Performance initially
tracked historical midterm election patterns before diverging meaningfully as the year
progressed, though there are still three months to go. Several items remain at hand as U.S.

voters head to the polls in November, including geopolitical and foreign policy uncertainty,
energy driven inflation pressures, affordability concerns, U.S. fiscal trajectory, and rapid
advancements in AI. Regardless of the eventual political outcome, history suggests that
markets and the economy can perform well across different configurations of government.
In our view, markets continue to be anchored by strong fundamentals and encouraging
seasonal trends.

To Be Addressed. U.S. domestic and foreign policy remain in focus as the global reorder,
tit-for-tat tariffs, and geopolitical conflicts spanning the Middle East to Eastern Europe have
added layers of uncertainty to the U.S. economic outlook.
At the same time, persistent geopolitical flare-ups have pushed oil prices materially
higher, adding to inflation pressures and keeping them above the Fed’s 2% target.
Headline Consumer Price Index (CPI) held above 3% year-over-year (YoY) in August, while

its core measure, which excludes food and energy, held at 2.4% YoY. The latest Personal
Consumption Expenditure (PCE), the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation, also remained
above 3% YoY for both its headline and core figures. 2
The U.S. fiscal trajectory is another area of focus. The U.S. budget deficit stands at 5.4% of
GDP , just above this century’s average of 4.6%. Meanwhile, U.S. public sector debt surpassed
the $40 trillion mark for the first time in August, oscillating around 100% of GDP .3 While these

trends warrant close monitoring, we view them as a slow-moving structural headwind rather
than an immediate concern, particularly within a highly innovative and diverse U.S. economy.
AI has emerged as a policy consideration, with debates increasingly centered on both
regulatory safeguards and the economic implications of the expanding infrastructure buildout.
For voters, those concerns extend to affordability, as the significant energy requirements of

new data centers raise questions about potential pressure on electricity costs.
Strap In for The Ride. Leading up to the midterm election, markets tend to experience
greater swings as investors navigate uncertainty surrounding the policy path for the
remainder of the presidential cycle (Exhibit 2A). Since 1930, the S&P 500 has averaged an
intrayear drawdown of 20% in midterm years, with the latest election in 2022 experiencing
a 25% intrayear pullback. This year, however, the S&P 500 largest peak-to-trough drawdown

has been 9%, considerably shallower than its long-term average (Exhibit 2B). Yet while
volatility may rise as election day approaches, 2026 has increasingly defied the traditional
midterm year playbook.
Why 2026 is Different. Despite entering a challenging stretch of the presidential cycle,
U.S. Equities have materially outpaced the typical year two pattern (Exhibit 2C). The S&P
500 experienced its best Q2 in a midterm year since 1938, surging 14.9% compared to an

average decline of 1.6%. From a monthly perspective, September stands out as the weakest
month, though month-to-date performance points to an unusual 1.0% rise. That resilience
follows a 2.6% gain in August, another month in which performance meaningfully exceeded
the historical decline of 0.5% (Exhibit 2D).
Through September, U.S. Equity resilience has also extended beyond headline returns with
both the frequency and magnitude of market pullbacks remaining relatively subdued. Market

corrections of 10% or more have occurred in just 29% of midterm election years since 1930.4
Against this backdrop, the market’s last correction came in spring 2025, while this year has
2 Bloomberg, Bureau of Labor Statistics. Data through August 31, 2026.

weakness could represent a

### Page 5

5 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
seen only one 5%+ pullback compared to the average three per year, underscoring relatively
resilient market conditions.5 Year-to-date, the S&P 500 has increased 13%, well above the
average annual return of 3% according to Bloomberg through September 25, and on pace
to mark the strongest midterm election year since 2006. Taken together, 2026 has thus far
defied the traditionally weak midterm year seasonal pattern, reinforcing that every election

cycle is unique and historical trends may be a useful guide to navigate market volatility.
What’s Ahead? Forward performance is increasingly constructive for Equities. In the
post-war period, the S&P 500 rose 100% of the time in the six and 12 months following
elections, averaging 13% and 14%, respectively. 6 Even narrowing the time frame to three
months, markets ended higher 90% of the time. As investors push through the finish line,
the last three months of the year tend to be the strongest with October standing out,

ending higher 67% of the time. On average, Q4 has gained 5.6% since 1930, far surpassing
the preceding three quarters of election years. 7 Greater clarity about the U.S. fiscal policy
path serves as a potential tailwind for investors following the election.
To Take Home. Volatility tends to rise during midterm years, but history suggests that
reacting to short-term political uncertainty has rarely rewarded long-term investors. Over
longer horizons, the data suggests Equity returns and economic strength are shaped less

by election outcomes than by corporate profits, valuations, labor market conditions and
capital investments.

THE TICKER TAPE
What is the bond market telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
We believe the bond market is telling us that the Fed is on track to meeting their dual
mandate of price stability and full employment. More importantly, when examining the
full yield curve the 2-year Treasury yield is currently around 4.88%, which typically is
the most sensitive to the fed funds rate and tends to price in future potential policy

adjustments. At the same time, the 10-year Treasury yield is currently about 5.11%. This
leads us to believe that the market expects the Fed to engineer a couple of more hikes
while the back end of the curve is indicating that economic growth is still healthy.
Taken together, it appears the full curve, which has clearly flattened, believes the Fed is
doing its job, while supply shocks, for obvious reasons, are not leading to higher long-run
inflation. The backup in long-dated yields is growth, not necessarily inflation as indicated

by rising “real” yields. Therefore, we view the drift higher in yields as a more normal sign
than what many have originally suggested. We continue to emphasize higher economic
growth for longer, rising productivity in the medium term, supply shortages to remain in
key areas within the AI space, and shocks in the energy equation. We expect the energy
shocks to ultimately fade away over time, but we do not believe that growth fades
anytime soon. In our view, a more normal bond market pricing in Fed moves at the short

end, and growth at the long end helps financial conditions and economic growth operate
in a more durable manner.
In summary, the front-end of the curve expects 3 to 4 more hikes in the next 18 months
or so. This can be debated obviously based on future data especially short-term energy
supply shocks. Long-end yields are drifting higher based on five main factors below, in
our view.
1. Strong economic growth (both
services and manufacturing)
2. Hawkish Fed

3. Debt and deficit concerns
4. Lower demand for long duration
sovereign debt in general
5. Energy supply shocks
The largest weight being strong economic activity which kick starts portfolio
repositioning particularly as the Fed hikes rates. The repositioning stops once demand
rises again when yields reach attractive levels for the more patient longer term
investment community. The supply shocks ultimately end and debt and deficit worries will
likely remain, but they still take a backseat for now. Longer duration government debt is

becoming more attractive each week even in the face of the concerns above. Be patient

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500 Index Price Return is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States. A price return index is a stock
market index that tracks only the price changes and capital gains or losses of its component securities, without including dividends or cash distributions.
S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index is an early monthly economic indicator that estimates overall private-sector business activity by combining manufacturing

Investing involves risk, including the possible loss of principal. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and
investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.

Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

UBND TP.HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng 
mạnh từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh 
trong thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự 
án của doanh nghiệp ở mảng này sẽ vận hành hiệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và 
lợi nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA với 
BWE, giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ vọng 42.2% so với 
giá đóng cửa ngày 22/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh 
 Biwase là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực cung cấp nước sạch với 
thị phần đạt 100% tại Bình Dương. 
Ngoài ra, công ty còn mở rộng sang 
lĩnh vực đầu tư nhà máy xử lý nước và 
chất thải rắn nhằm hoàn thiện hệ 
sinh thái dịch vụ hạ tầng môi trường 
tại khu vực. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Nắng nóng thúc đẩy tiêu thụ nước sạch khối dân cư, đem về tổng doanh thu cấp nước tại Bình Dương và 
Bình Phước năm 2026/2027 đạt 2,953/3,192 tỷ VND (6.8%/7.4%). 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Nhà máy nước Nhị Thành giai đoạn 3 đi vào hoạt động giúp tổng doanh thu nhà máy đạt lần lượt 213/247 tỷ 
VND (+16%/16% YoY) trong 2026/2027 và duy trì tăng trưởng trung bình 15% đến 2030. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước: 
tại đây

2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15%

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số 
 Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 
325 tỷ VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ). Mảng cung cấp nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp 
tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% 
QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025. Kinh doanh tăng trưởng 
tích cực là nhờ giá nước đầu vào mua từ TDM thấp hơn giúp tăng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp, cùng với không phát sinh lỗ tỷ giá như cùng kỳ năm ngoái.

### Page 4

4

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh 
được thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng 
nóng hơn; trong khi triển vọng 
nhóm khách hàng công nghiệp bị 
ảnh hưởng bởi bất lợi thuế quan 
 Dự báo đồng thuận xác suất xảy ra El Nino ở mức xấp xỉ 100% từ 2H2026 kéo 
dài đến tháng đầu 2027 cho thấy cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt 
nắng nóng sẽ gay gắt và kéo dài hơn so với các năm trước. Đây là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY 
(+2.6/1.1 ppts). Trái lại, sau khi section 301 được công bố và có hiệu lực thay 
thế section 122, tình hình thuế quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho 
các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống của Việt Nam. Cụ thể, Section 301 không tự 
hết hạn và có nền tảng pháp lý chắc chắn (ít nhất là trong nhiệm kỳ của 
Trump); đồng thời áp dụng hạn ngạch thuế quan cho một lượng hàng dệt may 
nhất định của một số nước không có Việt Nam, khiến bối cảnh bất lợi cho các 
ngành sản xuất truyền thống. Tuy nhiên, với việc Bình Dương đang tập trung 
đầu tư hạ tầng khu công nghiệp hiện đại nhằm thu hút dòng vốn FDI vào các 
lĩnh vực công nghệ cao, sản lượng nước sạch sẽ được duy trì khi tiêu thụ khối 
sản xuất truyền thống được bù đắp bởi nhóm công nghệ cao. Theo đó, trong 
năm 2026, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng nhẹ dự báo sản lượng nước sạch tại Bình 
Dương & Bình Phước đạt 224.9 triệu m3 (+6.8% YoY), với mức tăng của nhóm 
sản xuất/sinh hoạt/hành chính lần lượt là 5.8%/7.5%/11% YoY.

Biểu đồ 4. Xác suất tăng dần của EL Nino

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV tổng hợp

Mảng cấp nước: Tiến độ điều chỉnh 
giá bán nước gián đoạn do môi 
trường lạm phát leo cao 
 Do tác động của cuộc chiến Mỹ - Iran gây ra gián đoạn dòng chảy vận tải dầu 
tại eo biển Hormuz, giá nhiên liệu trong nước đã tăng mạnh từ đầu năm (Dầu 
Diesel - +64.3%, E5 RON 92 – +40%) và khiến cho tình hình lạm phát trong nước 
diễn biến tiêu cực: CPI bình quân 8 tháng năm 2026 tăng 4.5% với chỉ số giá 
nhóm nhà ở, điện nước, chất đốt và vật liệu xây dựng tăng cao nhất với 6.7% và 
giao thông tăng 5.4%. Trong bối cảnh đó, chúng tôi cho rằng tiến độ điều 
chỉnh giá bán nước tại các thị trường của BWE có khả năng bị đẩy lùi sang 
2027-2028 hoặc cho đến khi tình hình giá nhiên liệu ổn định trở lại. Trong dài 
hạn, khả năng đàm phán giá nước thành công là khá cao bởi: i) Thông tư 
145/2025/TT-BTC có hiệu lực từ 2026 là căn cứ để UBND quy định tỷ suất lợi 
nhuận định mức các dự án nhà máy nước; ii) BWE và cổ đông lớn là TDM dự 
kiến đầu tư và mở rộng công suất cung cấp – phân phối nước tại các vùng hẻo 
lánh nên có khả năng tăng áp lực đàm phán tăng giá nước tại Bình Dương. 
Trong kịch bản thận trọng, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng giá nước sạch trung bình khu

vực Bình Dương 1%/năm từ 2028 – 2030.

Đơn giá xử thu gom, vận chuyện và 
xử lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho 
phép cải thiện doanh thu và lợi 
nhuận của mảng này 
 UBND TP.HCM (bao gồm TP.HCM cũ, Bình Dương, Bà Rịa Vũng Tàu) quy định 
giá cụ thể đối với dịch vụ vận chuyển, xử lý chất thải rắn sinh hoạt trên địa bàn. 
Theo đó, quy định mới thay thế văn bản có hiệu lực từ 2016 sẽ chuyển hình 
thức tính giá đầy đủ theo 3 cấu phần: Thu gom, vận chuyển và Xử lý; với khung 
giá được xác định cho các phường đô thị và xã/nông thôn. So với quy định 
2016, quy định mới điều chỉnh tăng mạnh khung giá khoán đối với chủ nguồn 
thải nhỏ (Hộ gia đình tăng 80-184% đối với khu vực đô thị, 55-140% đối với khu 
vực nông thôn; Hộ kinh doanh nhỏ lẻ tăng 42-150% đối với khu vực đô thị, 20-
138% đối với khu vực nông thôn); đồng thời yêu cầu chủ thải tại nguồn và chủ 
nguồn thải lớn áp dụng khung giá thành (bao gồm giá thu gom, vận chuyển và 
xử lý) nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn chi phí liên quan tới các khâu xử 
lý rác thải thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng việc ban hành khung giá mới tính toán 
đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh phản ánh định hướng phát triển 
hạ tầng xử lý rác thải hiện đại của TP.HCM, đặt trọng tâm vào các nhà máy đốt 
rác phát điện thân thiện và an toàn với môi trường. Đối với doanh nghiệp hoạt 
động trong lĩnh vực môi trường nhiều kinh nghiệm như BWE, cơ chế thu gom, 
vận chuyển và xử lý minh bạch sẽ giúp thúc đẩy hiệu quả các dự án mà doanh 
nghiệp có kế hoạch triển khai trong tương lai. KBSV dự báo doanh thu và lợi 
nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Nguồn: UBND TP .HCM, KBSV

Biwase thành công tăng vốn cổ 
phần lên 2,513 tỷ VND 
 Sau đợt chào báo cho cổ đông hiện hữu với giá chào bán 37,000 đồng/cổ 
phiếu, doanh nghiệp dự kiến huy động được khoảng 1,162.5 tỷ VND, nâng vốn 
điều lệ lên mức 2,513 tỷ VND. Khoản vốn bổ sung được dự kiến sử dụng cho ba 
mục đích chính: thanh toán nợ vay đến hạn (khoảng 773.3 tỷ đồng), đầu tư dự 
án nhà máy điện rác công suất 24MW (225 tỷ đồng) và mua cổ phần tại CTCP 
Cấp nước Ninh Thuận (hơn 164 tỷ đồng), qua đó biến doanh nghiệp này trở 
thành công ty liên kết. Dự kiến, lợi nhuận đóng góp từ công ty liên kết trong 
2026/2027 tiếp tục tăng trưởng tích cực, đạt 238.1/245.2 tỷ VND (40%/3% YoY).

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Về diễn biến thị trường, BWE hiện đang giao dịch quanh mức -1 độ lệch chuẩn 
trung bình 5 năm EV/EBITDA, phản ánh những bất ổn trong thương mại toàn 
cầu ảnh hưởng tới tiêu thụ nước phục vụ sản xuất. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh 
giá triển vọng trong tương lai của mảng môi trường (bao gồm nước thải và rác 
thải) cộng với chiến lược đa dạng hóa địa bàn cung cấp nước sẽ bù đắp được 
KQKD tăng trưởng chậm lại.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA 
với BWE, giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ 
vọng 42.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 22/09/2026. Mức EV/EBITDA mục tiêu cho 
việc định giá doanh nghiệp là 8.8 lần, tương ứng với mức trung bình 5 năm của 
doanh nghiệp, để phản ánh sự cải thiện KQKD đến từ mảng môi trường và cơ 
chế giảm giá mua nước từ TDM sẽ bù đắp sự giảm tốc của mảng cấp nước trong 
ngắn hạn. Với mức giá mục tiêu trên, cổ phiếu được định giá tương đương P/B 
1.7x cho 2026, phù hợp với việc ROE được kỳ vọng cải thiện lên mức 14.1%.

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG September 4, 2026
WEEKLY AGGREGATES
ITEM DEFINITIONS AND METHODOLOGY
3© 2026 LSEG.  All marks herein are used under licence. TRPR_82222_867
General:
All aggregates are bottom-up aggregates calculated from analysts' consensus forecasts unless otherwise noted. Bottom-up
aggregates are weighted calculations using the I/B/E/S data from LSEG share class monitored or the official index weight of
the index-provider. Top-down forecasts are compilations of strategists' estimates. I/B/E/S universe calculations are based on

all companies within a given market monitored by LSEG.
Benchmark index calculations, including those for official local market indices, the S&P 500 Index, and the FTSE World Equity
Indices are calculated using official index constituents and weightings. All index composites are representative of current
index constituents. Index constituents and weightings are updated monthly during options expiration (the Thursday before the
third Friday of every month) for every index except the S&P 500, the constituents of which are current as of the date of

calculation.
PEIGBY refers to the Price Earnings Inverse Government Bond Yield ratio, which measures equity valuation with respect to
the inverse-yield on long-term government bonds (now known as the 'Fed' model). All PEIGBY ratios are I/B/E/S universe-
specific except for the US ratio, where it is based on the S&P 500 Index.
Methodology:
For US and selected Canadian aggregates, calendar-year aggregation is performed using the sum of four quarters' estimates

and a quarterly pro-ration is applied to non-December year end companies' quarterly EPS estimates. Outside of the US and
Canada, all company-level data are calendarized prior to aggregation using the Compustat method of calendarization.
Aggregation is performed using the annual consensus EPS estimate and a May roll-back is applied to December year-end
markets. Aggregates for Japan, where most companies report in March, use an August rollback and a November rollback is

used for Australia and New Zealand, where the fiscal year-end for most companies is in June.
All 12-month-forward data for ex-US markets are calculated using a pro-rata weighting to annual EPS forecasts. The
weightings applied are a daily calibration out of 365 days relative to each constituent's individual fiscal year-end and the
current date.
All non-surprise data, including aggregate forecast earnings, forecast growth, and forward-looking valuation calculations are

weighted according to the I/B/E/S share class monitored for I/B/E/S universes or the weightings assigned to each constituent
in a given index. Aggregated earnings calculations are the equivalent of net profit aggregates, as they are derived from the
EPS forecasts aggregated to the net income level by the appropriate share weighting.
Earnings Aggregation:
For 12-month-forward earnings, the 12-month-forward EPS is used in place of the calendarized EPS in the formulas below.

I/B/E/S universe EPS Aggregate = Index EPS Aggregate =
Growth Calculations:
All growth calculations are the weighted year over year growth in earnings for the calendarized fiscal year period. The
formulas below use index shares for index growth calculations.
Calendarized Earnings Growth = 12-month-forward Growth =
Valuation:
All valuation calculations are the weighted-average price-earnings ratio for the fiscal period in question. The formulas below

use index shares for index P/E calculations.
Calendarized P/E Aggregate = 12-month-forward P/E =
Revisions Momentum:
Unless otherwise indicated, analysts' revisions momentum is a simple aggregation of the total number of estimates raised
divided by the total number of estimates lowered. Thus, any revisions ratio less than 1 indicates negative revisions
momentum while a ratio greater than 1 indicates positive revisions momentum, with 1 marking parity.

The Global Aggregates from LSEG product contains annual aggregates for universes included in this publication, including
supplemental aggregate data of over 100 items as well as history dating back to 1985 for North America and to 1987 for other
markets. To learn more about this product, contact your LSEG sales representative.
FTSE and the FTSE World Equity Indices are owned by FTSE International Limited.

## Document 245: PineDrama下载破亿，字节想在海外再造一个红果

Source PDF: all report pdf/PineDrama下载破亿，字节想在海外再造一个红果.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

字节跳动将红果免费短剧模式复制出海，旗下独⽴短剧 App PineDrama 上线不⾜⼀年下载
量破亿，⽇活 700 万，靠 TikTok 流量优势与本⼟原创内容在印尼、巴西强势突围。然⽽，⽤
⼾规模狂飙背后，⼴告变现能否跑通仍是最⼤悬念 —— 免费⼊场易，盈利收场难。
摘要：
字节跳动正在把国内短剧市场的成熟打法，搬到海外。
2026 年初，字节旗下独⽴短剧 App PineDrama 上线，采⽤与红果相似的免费模式。
不到⼀年，累计下载量超过 1 亿，⽇活约 700 万。 2026 年⼆季度， PineDrama 在 Sensor Tower 中
国⾮游戏应⽤出海下载榜中排名第七，也是今年海外下载破亿的七款短剧 App 中唯⼀的新⾯孔。
在此之前，海外短剧市场主要由 ReelShort 、 DramaBox 等平台占据，并且普遍采⽤前⼏集免
费、后续单集付费的模式。 PineDrama 却靠免费模式快速起量，在原有市场格局中撕开了⼀道
⼝⼦。
它为什么能这么快做起来？红果在国内跑通的这套免费模式玩法，到了海外还能继续奏效吗？

PineDrama 为什么能快速起量？
PineDrama 的增⻓，⾸先和它采⽤的免费模式有关。
此前海外短剧平台主要依靠付费解锁，⽤⼾看完前⼏集后，需要继续付费才能观看，部分平台
的周订阅费⽤甚⾄超过 20 美元。这种模式虽然可以直接获得⽤⼾付费，但也会将价格敏感⽤⼾
挡在⻔外，影响整体⽤⼾的增⻓。
PineDrama 把内容免费开放之后，覆盖到的⽤⼾范围⾃然更⼤。尤其是在印尼、巴西等付费习
惯较弱的新兴市场，免费更容易被⽤⼾接受，这让它有机会从原有付费市场之外找到新的⽤⼾
增量。
Sensor Tower 数据显⽰，截⽌今年 7 ⽉印尼和巴西合计贡献 PineDrama82% 的累计下载量。具体
来看， AppMagic 数据显⽰， PineDrama 是 8 ⽉份巴西下载量最⾼的应⽤程序，并且连续第⼆个
⽉蝉联榜⾸。
除了免费模式，字节在流量分发上的优势，也是 PineDrama 快速起量的重要原因。
PineDrama 可以使⽤ TikTok 账号⼀键登录，产品交互也接近短视频应⽤。上线初期，它可以利⽤

字节已有的流量和分发能⼒获取⽤⼾，不需要完全依赖传统短剧平台外部买量。
对于短剧平台来说，流量成本直接影响增⻓能不能持续。普通平台需要投⼊⼤量资⾦买量，才
能把内容推到⽤⼾⾯前。 PineDrama 背靠字节，可以利⽤ TikTok 的⽤⼾和流量资源，这让它在获
取⽤⼾时拥有更⼤的空间。
流量带来的⽤⼾规模还需要内容承接，这也是 PineDrama 增⻓的另⼀个动⼒。
PineDrama 没有把国内短剧简单翻译之后推向海外，⽽是以海外本⼟原创为主。⽐如《 10 亿奈
拉新娘》由⾹港团队制作，故事采⽤尼⽇利亚本⼟背景，上线⼀周播放量突破 4000 万，累计超
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

过 6000 万。《印尼豪⻔⻛云》《⾥约救赎》等本⼟题材，播放量也都突破 2 亿。
海外短剧的竞争正在从内容出海转向本⼟创作。早期，中国短剧可以依靠成熟的制作体系，将
国内内容翻译、配⾳后推向海外。但随着市场不断扩⼤，单纯依靠语⾔转换已经不够，平台还
需要理解不同市场的⽂化背景和⽤⼾偏好，创作更贴近当地⽣活的故事。本⼟原创，正在成为
短剧平台争夺海外⽤⼾的重要⼀环。
先⽤免费模式和 TikTok 资源扩⼤⽤⼾覆盖，再⽤本⼟内容承接观看需求，构成了 PineDrama ⽬前
⽐较清晰的增⻓路径。
免费模式在海外到底赚不赚钱？
把⽤⼾规模做起来之后， PineDrama ⾯临的考验才真正开始。
现在海外短剧市场已经出现了⼀个明显的错位。 Owl & Co. 数据显⽰，免费短剧 App 的⽤⼾时⻓
占⽐，在五个季度⾥从约 15% 升⾄约 83% 。但 MPA 测算， 2026 年中国以外的短剧收⼊规模约 36
亿美元，收⼊仍然⾼度集中在 ReelShort 、 DramaBox 等付费平台，两家合计约占⼀半。

⽤⼾越来越愿意看免费内容，市场⾥的主要收⼊却还来⾃付费平台。这说明，免费模式已经证
明了获取⽤⼾和观看时⻓的能⼒，但⼴告变现还没有完全跟上。
PineDrama ⾯对的就是这个问题。
它⽬前的下载主要来⾃印尼、巴西等新兴市场，这些地区的⽤⼾变现价值相对较低，⽽北美等
⾼价值市场占⽐有限。⽤⼾可以贡献⼤量观看时⻓，但平台最终能从这些时⻓中获得多少⼴告
收⼊，还要看当地⼴告市场的成熟程度。
红果的经验可以提供⼀个参照。
红果在国内能够跑通免费短剧，有⼀个重要前提，就是⽤⼾规模⾜够⼤，同时⼴告市场也⽐较
成熟。红果⽇活约 1.68 亿，⼈均每天使⽤时⻓约 125 分钟，庞⼤的⽤⼾时⻓能够形成⾜够的⼴告
库存，国内⼴告市场也有能⼒承接这些流量。
相⽐之下， PineDrama ⽬前的⽤⼾规模和市场分布，还不⾜以证明它已经具备同样的商业化条
件。
因此，对字节来说， PineDrama 接下来的重点，是在扩⼤⽤⼾规模的同时，逐步提升⼴告变现
效率。免费模式可以降低观看⻔槛，本⼟化内容则有助于吸引不同市场的⽤⼾，但这些优势最

终能否转化为稳定收⼊，还需要看平台能否持续满⾜⽤⼾的内容需求，并建⽴起与⽤⼾规模相
匹配的商业化能⼒。
下载量破亿，证明了免费模式在海外获取⽤⼾的潜⼒。但是，要把⽤⼾增⻓变成⼀⻔可持续的
⽣意， PineDrama 还需要跨过商业化这⼀关。
免费⼊局后海外短剧⾛向分化
随着免费模式的兴起，海外短剧原有的竞争格局也开始出现变化。
⾸先看原来的付费市场。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
164 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
ReelShort 、 DramaBox 等头部付费平台并没有被迅速冲垮。 MPA 预测， ReelShort 2026 年营收
约 10.5 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 34% ，并⾸次实现⼤规模盈利。与此同时，五强之外约有 300 个中⼩
App 只能分⻝剩余约 21% 的市场份额。
免费模式带来的压⼒，更多落在中腰部平台和依赖买量获取⽤⼾的玩家⾝上。头部付费平台已
经积累了⽤⼾、内容和商业化能⼒，仍然可以依靠成熟的付费模式获得收⼊，⽽中⼩平台既要
承担获客成本，⼜要⾯对⽤⼾付费意愿可能下降的问题，经营压⼒会更⼤。
其次，免费模式本⾝也正在进⼊竞争加剧的阶段。
Sensor Tower 数据显⽰， 2026 年下载量破亿的七款海外短剧 App 中，已经有三款以免费模式为

主， FreeReels 、 Melolo 等产品也在扩⼤免费短剧⽤⼾规模。与此同时， AI 持续降低内容⽣产成
本，免费内容的供给还会继续增加。
随着玩家的持续进⼊和技术的不断迭代，免费本⾝已经很难形成明显差异，平台还要持续投⼊
内容和流量去争夺⽤⼾。对于没有⼤型流量⼊⼝的中⼩平台，免费虽然更容易吸引⽤⼾，但平
台仍然要付出不少获客成本，后续还要依靠⼴告把⽤⼾规模转化成收⼊，经营压⼒预计会不断
加⼤。
市场也可能因此逐渐形成两种不同的⽣意。
⼀种主要⾯向北美等⾼付费市场，通过单集付费、订阅等⽅式获得收⼊。这类市场的⽤⼾直接
付费能⼒更强，平台依靠较⾼的单⽤⼾收⼊获得商业回报，市场也会继续向少数头部平台集
中。
另⼀种主要⾯向印尼、巴西等新兴市场，以免费内容扩⼤⽤⼾规模，再通过⼴告完成商业化。
这种模式对流量⼊⼝和⼴告系统的要求更⾼，更适合拥有⼤型流量平台和⼴告业务的公司。红
果已经在国内验证了免费短剧的商业模式。
PineDrama 正在把这套逻辑放到海外。只是海外新兴市场的⼴告价值还需要进⼀步提升，免费

模式能否从⽤⼾增⻓⾛向稳定盈利仍有待验证。

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Shifted the focus back to energy power vendors again

Shifted the focus back to energy power vendors again

Shifted the focus back to
energy power vendors again
在⾕歌與 Constellation 簽署兩份⻑期合約後，市場明顯將注意⼒轉
回電⼒能源產業
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 6 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
What’s New?
Google 與美國最⼤核電業者  Constellation Energy 達成指標性⻑期採購協
議，簽署  20 年期  43 億美元核能⼤單，總供電承諾達  3,590 MW 。

雙軌合約結構（增容  PPA + ⻑期基載電⼒）：
890 MW 功率增容  PPA：為期  20 年， Google 直接資助  Constellation 位
於伊利諾州、賓州與紐澤⻄州的  11 座現有核電機組進⾏翻新與技術升級（更
換蒸汽發⽣器、渦輪機與控制系統），相當於憑空增添⼀座⼤型反應爐的發電
容量，預計  2028 年起陸續供電⾄  2033 年全⾯上線。
2,700 MW 電網電⼒供應協議：為期  15 年，鎖定  PJM 電網內的穩定電⼒配
額（請參考附註了解 PJM 電網架構），為  Constellation 機組提供⻑期現⾦流
保障。
開創「⾃帶電⼒（ Bring Your Own Power, BYOP ）」防禦機制：針對資
料中⼼擴建引發「科技巨頭推⾼⺠⽣電費」的批評，本案採⽤  PJM 電網架
構，由  Google ⾃掏腰包承擔核電站設備升級的巨額資本⽀出，主打不將成本
轉嫁給⼀般居⺠⽤電戶。
科技巨頭核能策略可能轉向「既有設施增容與重啟」：繼微軟重啟三哩島

（ Three Mile Island ）、 Meta 採購  Clinton 核電廠電⼒、亞⾺遜合作  Talen

### Page 2

Energy 後， Google 也從⻑遠的  SMR （⼩型模組化反應爐）進⼀步落實到即
時可⾏的「既有核電廠增容」與「退役電廠重啟」路線。

What companies can be beneﬁted and why?
核電與獨⽴發電業者（ Independent Power Producers, IPP ）：
Constellation Energy (CEG)：最直接受益者，獲得確定性極⾼的  43 億
美元投資承諾與  20 年⻑期現⾦流，⼤幅提升估值倍數。（請參考之前深度報

data center expansion? 8 names in comparison ）
Vistra (VST) 、 Talen Energy (TLNE) 、 PSEG：全美⾮受監管核電與基

載電⼒資產被全⾯重估，從過去低成⻑的公⽤事業轉型為  AI 時代具戰略溢價

重型發電與核電設備供應商（ Power & Industrial OEMs ）：

（ LEU ）的⻑期結構性需求更加穩固。
超⼤規模雲端巨頭（ Google / Alphabet ）：
在  PJM 這個全美資料中⼼最密集且電網負載最吃緊的區域，提前鎖定⻑達  20

年的無碳基載電⼒，避免因電網壅塞⽽被迫延後  AI 伺服器機房上線，同時捍
衛其  2030 年  24/7 零碳排（ CFE ）的承諾。

缺乏儲能的純綠能業者（ Pure-play Wind / Solar Developers ）：

### Page 3

AI 資料中⼼要求  99.999% 的  24/7 恆定供電。純⾵光發電的間歇性在科技巨
頭預算配置中逐漸失寵，科技公司的購電優先順序正轉向核能與地熱，傳統未
配儲能的純光伏 / ⾵電開發商的議價空間遭到壓縮，除⾮再配合儲能系統⼀起打
包出售。
⼆線雲端業者與中⼩規模資料中⼼業者（ Tier-2 CSPs & Mid-size
Colocations ）：
電⼒正取代晶⽚成為算⼒擴張的最⼤瓶頸。巨頭憑藉數⼗億美元級別的資產負
債表包下龐⼤發電量，⼆線廠商在  PJM 等熱⾨電網拿不到⾜夠並網容量，⾯
臨嚴峻的「電⼒排擠效應」。
區域內⾼耗能的傳統製造業（鋼鐵、重化⼯業）：
儘管增容部分的建設由  Google 出資，但⾼達  2,700 MW 的電⼒合約仍鎖定
了電網中龐⼤的供給資源。隨著整體區域備載容量緊縮， PJM 容量拍賣
（ Capacity Auction ）價格⽔漲船⾼，可能進⼀步推⾼當地傳統⼯廠的⽤電
成本。
Our outside-the-box thinking
「核電增容」正在實質截胡  SMR （⼩型模組化反應爐）的⿈⾦窗⼝期：

市場共識普遍認為  SMR 是解決  AI 供電的終極救星，但  SMR ⾯臨審批週期
⻑、⾸台商業化成本過⾼（ FOAK ⾵險）的挑戰。 Constellation 此次透過
11 座現有反應爐增容  890 MW ，直⾔這相當於「在更短時間內憑空建出好幾
座  SMR 」。這意味著在  2028 〜 2035 年間，「增容  + 重啟舊機組」⽬前是科
技業解決⽅案，甚⾄可能使部分商業化進度落後的  SMR 新創公司⾯臨資⾦被
抽離的⽣存危機。
「⾃帶電⼒（ BYOP ）」表⾯上避免推⾼⺠⽣電價，實質上在將公共電網「階
級私有化」：
儘管科技業標榜⾃費升級電廠不影響⼀般⽤電戶，但背後本質是頂級科技資本
以  20 年⻑約包下全美最優質、最穩定的無碳基載發電能⼒。當優質核電被矽
⾕巨頭鎖定，公共電網與⼀般⼤眾將被迫分攤⾼波動、依賴天然氣或間歇性⾵
光的發電⾵險。這在未來數年極可能引發嚴重的監管審查與社會反壟斷反彈
（ Regulatory & Social Backlash ）。
科技巨頭正演變為「影⼦綜合能源公⽤事業（ Shadow Utilities ）」：

Google 這次不是被動向公⽤事業買電，⽽是扮演「電廠重⼤資本改造的出資
⽅兼推⼿」。科技公司的資產負債表已直接穿透⾄發電基礎設施底層。未來衡

### Page 4

量⼀間  AI 超⼤規模雲端巨頭的核⼼壁壘，可能不再單看演算法參數或  GPU 庫
存，⽽是「能源專案融資與物理鎖定基載電⼒的能⼒」。
極端能源溢價將反向倒逼「算⼒能效⾰命」與「端側 / 邊緣  AI 」超預期爆發：
當鎖定幾座資料中⼼的電⼒需動輒砸下  40~50 億美元與  20 年承諾時，雲端
暴⼒堆疊參數量（ Brute-force Scaling ）的邊際經濟效益正在逼近物理極
限。這將逼迫產業加速尋找替代⽅案：更極致的稀疏化模型（ MoE ）、光學計
算晶⽚，以及能將計算負載分散⾄終端設備的「邊緣 / 端側  AI （ Edge AI ）」，
這反⽽可能成為下⼀輪顛覆雲端資料中⼼商業模式的⾮共識催化劑。
附註：什麼是  PJM 電網架構
PJM （ PJM Interconnection ）  是全美規模最⼤、運作機制最成熟的區域輸電
組織（ RTO, Regional Transmission Organization ），負責調度與協調美國
東部及中⻄部  13 個州以及華盛頓特區的⾼壓電網與電⼒批發市場。

其名稱源⾃最初結盟的三個州： Pennsylvania （賓⼣法尼亞）、 Jersey （紐澤
⻄）、 Maryland （⾺⾥蘭）。如今，包含北維吉尼亞州（全球最密集的資料中
⼼聚落  Data Center Alley ）都在  PJM 的管轄範圍內。
⼀、  PJM 的組織⾓⾊與定位
PJM 本⾝不擁有電廠、不擁有電網，也不從事電⼒買賣。它是⼀個受美國聯邦
能源監管委員會（ FERC ）監管的獨⽴⾮營利實體，核⼼⾓⾊有⼆：
電網可靠度調度員（ Grid Reliability Operator ）：控制超過  84,000 英⾥的
⾼壓輸電線路，秒級平衡區域內  6,500 萬⼈的⽤電需求與發電供給。
批發電⼒市場管理者（ Market Operator ）：營運透明、競爭性的電⼒批發市
場，媒合獨⽴發電廠（如核電、燃氣、⾵光發電商）與電⼒零售商 / ⼤⽤電戶。
⼆、  PJM 的三⼤市場架構
PJM 的電⼒市場運作主要依賴三⼤核⼼機制：
電能市場（ Energy Market ）：

分為⽇前市場（ Day-Ahead ）與即時市場（ Real-Time ）。
採⽤分區邊際定價（ LMP, Locational Marginal Pricing ）。 LMP 不僅反映發
電成本，還包含輸電線路壅塞成本（ Congestion ）與線路損耗（ Losses ）。
若某區域（如維吉尼亞機房區）⽤電需求暴增且輸電線路壅塞，當地的  LMP
就會飆⾼。

### Page 5

容量市場（ Capacity Market / RPM, Reliability Pricing Model ）：
這是  PJM 最具特⾊的機制。為確保「未來  3 年」電網在尖峰負載時絕不斷
電， PJM 每年舉⾏容量基礎拍賣（ BRA, Base Residual Auction ）。
發電廠競標承諾在未來特定年份提供⾜夠的備⽤容量，得標者即使不發電，也
能按⽇領取「保證在場費」。近來因⽼舊電廠退役與  AI 負載激增， PJM 容量拍
賣清算價格屢創新⾼。
輔助服務市場（ Ancillary Services Market ）：
採購調頻（ Frequency Regulation ）、備轉容量（ Spinning Reserves ）等
服務，確保電網在發電機跳機或負載突增時，能在數秒⾄數分鐘內維持電網頻
率（ 60Hz ）穩定。
三、  為什麼  AI 巨頭與核電交易都繞不開  PJM ？
當前科技巨頭（微軟、 Google 、 Meta 、亞⾺遜）的核電採購與⼤型資料中⼼
擴建，⾼度集中在  PJM 範圍內，主因在於其獨特的戰略位置與市場結構：

考，並⾮投資建議，個⼈應審慎評估⾵險，如因此造成任何損失，本⼈不負任
何擔保責任 ; We are here to provide our professional opinions on
company and industry analysis but not the investment advice. The

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
The US IPO market has reopened in a big way in 2026, with issuance already at a
record high. But this activity has raised two key concerns for the equity market:
whether it’s flashing a la te-cycle warning sign and whether the market can
comfortably digest so much new supply. So, how worried should investors be? The
IPO Initiative’s Jay Ritter, Acadian’s Owen Lamont, and GS’ Ben Snider agree that
we’re not in an “IPO wave” (yet) but differ on ho w much of a red flag the surge in

issuance is signaling. Snider argues that the late-cycle warning signs of past IPO
booms aren’t evident today and digestion concerns are overdone. Ritter notes that
while high new issuance volume has historically predicted lower future returns, the
signal has been weak. But Lamont is more cautious, calling an IPO wave one of the
“four horsemen” of a bubble (especially alongside a surge in debt issuance), but
stresses that IPO waves don’t necessarily signal the market top. Key to watch: firs t

day IPO returns and the all-important AI outlook.
Ben Snider, Chief US Equity Strategist, Goldman Sachs
“I’m less worried [about the surge in new issuance] than the average investor… the late-cycle warning signs that have
historically accompanied IPO booms aren’t evident today… [and] the equity supply-demand balance this year looks better
than most investors intuit.”
Jay Ritter, Director of The IPO Initiative and Professor Emeritus, The University of Florida’s Warrington College

of Business
“High new issue volume is a predictor of low future market returns. But, like most indicators, this signal works only slightly
better than chance… So, I wouldn’t view an IPO boom on its own as a sign that a market downturn is imminent.”
Owen Lamont, Senior Vice President and Portfolio Manager, Acadian Asset Management
“Historically, issuance waves paired with capex waves… have generally resulted in disappointing returns for equity holders. So,
